I hate to say it, because I really am so blessed to have my family and my little home. I don't want to sound like I'm complaining, you know? \n\nBut yes, even when they bring me a nice cup of tea or show me a lovely drawing the grandchildren made, I just... I smile and say thank you, because I know it's a beautiful thing, but the feeling inside just doesn't quite match. It stays like that pretty much all day. I try to shake it off, I really do. Oh, doctor... I feel terrible, because I know I just told you I still care about my routines, and I do, I really do... but... well, I suppose if I'm being completely honest with you... and I really want to be a good patient and give you the right answers... I have noticed something.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, dear... I'm so sorry, doctor. I know you just asked me about this, and I... I must not have explained myself very well at all. Please forgive me, I don't mean to be difficult or make you repeat yourself.
